style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',sans-serif;font-size:12pt;font-weight:400;line-height:113%">DEFINITIONS AND INCORPORATION BY REFERENCE</font></div><div style="margin-bottom:10.6pt;margin-top:12.1pt;padding-left:100.8pt;padding-right:7.2pt;text-indent:36pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',sans-serif;font-size:12pt;font-weight:400;line-height:115%">Section 1.1. </font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',sans-serif;font-size:12pt;font-weight:400;line-height:115%;text-decoration:underline">Definitions</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',sans-serif;font-size:12pt;font-weight:400;line-height:115%">. Certain capitalized terms used herein (including the preamble and the recitals hereto) shall have the following meanings&#58;</font></div><div style="margin-bottom:0.05pt;margin-top:1.35pt;padding-left:30.75pt;padding-right:7.2pt;text-align:justify;text-indent:36pt"><font style="color:#0000ff;font-family:'Times New Roman',sans-serif;font-size:12pt;font-weight:400;line-height:115%;text-decoration:underline">&#8220;ABR&#8221; means, for any day, a rate per annum equal to the highest of (a) the Prime Rate in effect on such day, (b) the Federal Funds Rate in effect on such day plus 0.50% and (c) Adjusted Daily Simple SOFR in effect on such day plus 1.0%. Any change in the ABR due to a change in the Prime Rate, the Federal Funds Rate or Daily Simple SOFR shall be effective from and including the effective date of such change in the Prime Rate, the Federal Funds Rate or Daily Simple SOFR, respectively. To the extent that Daily Simple SOFR is not then available, clause (c) shall not apply.</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',sans-serif;font-size:12pt;font-weight:400;line-height:115%;text-decoration:underline"> </font></div><div style="margin-top:0.6pt;padding-left:32.4pt;padding-right:7.2pt;text-align:justify;text-indent:36pt"><font><br></font></div><div style="margin-top:0.6pt;padding-left:32.4pt;padding-right:7.2pt;text-align:justify;text-indent:36pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',sans-serif;font-size:12pt;font-weight:400;line-height:115%">&#8220;</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',sans-serif;font-size:12pt;font-weight:400;line-height:115%;text-decoration:underline">Account</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',sans-serif;font-size:12pt;font-weight:400;line-height:115%">&#8221; means each open-end revolving credit card account, that is identified as an Initial Account or an Additional Account. The term &#8220;Account&#8221; also includes each account into which an Account is transferred (a &#8220;</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',sans-serif;font-size:12pt;font-weight:400;line-height:115%;text-decoration:underline">Transferred Account</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',sans-serif;font-size:12pt;font-weight:400;line-height:115%">&#8221;) so long as such Transferred Account (i) has been transferred in accordance with the Credit and Collection Policy and (ii) can be traced or identified, by reference to or by way of any Account Schedule delivered to Depositor or the Depositor Receivables Trustee for the benefit of Depositor and Issuer, as an account into which an Account has been transferred. Any Account that becomes a Defaulted Account shall cease to be an Account for all purposes other than the calculation of Recoveries, and no existing balance or future charges on such account shall be deemed to be Transferred Receivables notwithstanding any subsequent reaffirmation of such account by the Obligor and any resulting action by the related Account Owner. The term Account includes an Additional Account only from and after its Addition Date and excludes any Removed Account after its Removal Date.</font></div><div style="margin-top:12.2pt;padding-left:32.4pt;padding-right:7.2pt;text-align:justify;text-indent:36pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',sans-serif;font-size:12pt;font-weight:400;line-height:115%">&#8220;</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',sans-serif;font-size:12pt;font-weight:400;line-height:115%;text-decoration:underline">Account Agreement</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',sans-serif;font-size:12pt;font-weight:400;line-height:115%">&#8221; means with respect to an Account, the agreement by and between the Account Owner and the Obligor thereof governing the terms and conditions of such Account, as such agreement may be amended, restated, supplemented or otherwise modified from time to time.</font></div><div style="margin-top:12.2pt;padding-left:32.4pt;padding-right:7.2pt;text-align:justify;text-indent:36pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',sans-serif;font-size:12pt;font-weight:400;line-height:115%">&#8220;</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',sans-serif;font-size:12pt;font-weight:400;line-height:115%;text-decoration:underline">Account Owner</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',sans-serif;font-size:12pt;font-weight:400;line-height:115%">&#8221; means, with respect to any Account, (i) the Initial Originator, or any other entity that, pursuant to a Program Agreement related to such Account, is the issuer of the credit cards related to, or the owner of, such Account, and (ii) if such Account is transferred to a successor Account Owner, such successor Account Owner.</font></div><div style="margin-top:0.15pt;padding-left:68.4pt;padding-right:57.6pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',sans-serif;font-size:12pt;font-weight:400;line-height:215%">&#8220;</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',sans-serif;font-size:12pt;font-weight:400;line-height:215%;text-decoration:underline">Account Schedule</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',sans-serif;font-size:12pt;font-weight:400;line-height:215%">&#8221; has the meaning set forth in the Transfer Agreement. &#8220;</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',sans-serif;font-size:12pt;font-weight:400;line-height:215%;text-decoration:underline">Addition Cut-Off Date</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',sans-serif;font-size:12pt;font-weight:400;line-height:215%">&#8221; has the meaning set forth in the Transfer Agreement. &#8220;</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',sans-serif;font-size:12pt;font-weight:400;line-height:215%;text-decoration:underline">Addition Date</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',sans-serif;font-size:12pt;font-weight:400;line-height:215%">&#8221; has the meaning set forth in the Transfer Agreement.</font></div><div style="height:49.68pt;position:relative;width:100%"><div style="bottom:0;position:absolute;width:100%"><div><table style="border-collapse:collapse;display:inline-table;margin-bottom:5pt;vertical-align:text-bottom;width:43.108%"><tr><td style="width:1.0%"></td><td style="width:98.900%"></td><td style="width:0.1%"></td></tr><tr><td colspan="3" style="padding:2px 1pt;text-align:left;vertical-align:bottom"><div><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',sans-serif;font-size:8pt;font-weight:400;line-height:125%">LEGAL_US_E # 170681720.5</font></div><div><font><br></font></div></td></tr></table></div></div></div><hr style="page-break-after:always"><div style="min-height:72pt;width:100%"><div><font><br></font></div></div><div style="margin-top:12.1pt;padding-left:32.4pt;padding-right:7.2pt;text-align:justify;text-indent:36pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',sans-serif;font-size:12pt;font-weight:400;line-height:115%">&#8220;</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',sans-serif;font-size:12pt;font-weight:400;line-height:115%;text-decoration:underline">Additional Accounts</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',sans-serif;font-size:12pt;font-weight:400;line-height:115%">&#8221;
